# Tasks

## AH. M3: the first fight, earned and kept on the kid's profile

No change-wide gate (design, Migration Plan): each box names what it stands on, AG's boxes by id, and the boxes run in the order those conditions allow. AH.1-AH.3 stand on AA alone; AH.6 and AH.12 on nothing in flight; the oracle boxes on AF's landed work and AG.1 and AG.7, and any use of an O5 trace on AG.34 and AG.35; the VM on AH.12 and AG's controller, props and locomotion; the slices on the TV on AG.20 and AG.21. Where a box shares files with a lane in flight (AG's in `guac-formats`, `guac-extract`, `guac-motion`, `guac-oracle`, `tools/oracle-harness` and the TV; AE's open boxes on the TV), whichever lands second keeps the other's tests passing. chotchki's answers gate no box before AH.28: each Open question's recommendation is what the boxes follow until he answers (design, Open Questions).

- [x] AH.1 The figure store (design decision 8), on AA's profile store and nothing of AG's: a migration in guacamole's own set ([`crates/guac-server/migrations/`](../../../crates/guac-server/migrations/)) adding `figure_instances` (`profile_id` → `profiles` on delete cascade, `figure_id` as `figure_usage` keys it, `xp` as the total, `gold`, `playtime_secs`, `generation`, `created_at`, `updated_at`; key (profile, figure); no CHECK on the caps); the store's queries (fresh, read, write absolute values clamped at the caps the caller passes, set, delete, bump the generation) on guacamole's arm only; the row made fresh at a profile's first placement beside `record_figure_usage` ([`profiles.rs`](../../../crates/guac-server/src/profiles.rs)), none for a placement with no owner; the native `Placement` ([`crates/rpcs3-control/src/lib.rs`](../../../crates/rpcs3-control/src/lib.rs)) carrying the instance's progress and generation, none without an owner (the product's drivers never build one); the TV-to-server path: a latest-wins map per (profile, figure) of absolute experience, gold and playtime with their generation, drained by the server thread, experience and gold written at once, playtime every 60 s of play, the figure's entry flushed when it leaves the portal, when the level ends and when guacamole quits, an entry under an older generation than the row's dropped; `FigureUpdated` broadcast when the level or the gold changes and with each playtime write, never per second. Unit tests in this box: fresh on a first placement and untouched on a second; one figure on two profiles is two rows; the caller's caps clamp; deleting a profile takes its rows; an ownerless placement makes no row and carries no progress; a burst of updates faster than the server drains, then a quit, keeping the final values; a quit straight after an orb keeping it; a removal flushing; an entry under the old generation dropped after an edit and after a reset; an experience change under the next level broadcasting nothing, a gold change broadcasting once and a minute of play broadcasting once; the database reopened keeps the row; the product's migration set has no such table and the query test over both sets still passes. Verify: `cargo test --workspace`; the product's phone e2e on the Mac at no fewer passes than AH's base (the store and the placement type are shared) (2026-10-19, M3 Max Mac: [`20261019000001_figure_instances.sql`](../../../crates/guac-server/migrations/20261019000001_figure_instances.sql) in guacamole's set adds `figure_instances` with the box's columns and key (`xp`, `gold`, `playtime_secs` and `generation` INTEGER NOT NULL, `created_at` and `updated_at` TEXT, no CHECK) and one table the box didn't name, `figure_generation`, a one-row counter every generation comes from: a reset deletes the row, and the row its next placement makes must never reuse a generation the TV may still be sending under. The queries are `ProfileStore::instances()` ([`instances.rs`](../../../crates/guac-server/src/instances.rs)): `fresh` (the row, made with zeros and a new generation when missing, an existing one returned untouched), `read`, `write` (the TV's absolute values clamped at the caller's `Caps`, playtime only when told, in one `UPDATE ... RETURNING` that matches only while the sent generation is no older than the row's), `set` (the editor's: experience and gold clamped, playtime kept, a new generation in the same transaction, the row made when missing), `delete` and `bump_generation`. The row is made in the worker's identity placement ([`state.rs`](../../../crates/guac-server/src/state.rs) `build_placement`) rather than in `load_slot` beside `record_figure_usage`, so a load, a resume, a restore and a Kaos swap all make it; that path runs only without the `FigureFiles` hook, so the product's database never sees a query, and a row that can't be made fails the placement as the product's working-copy failure does. `PlacementOwner` carries `progress: FigureProgress` (`xp`, `gold`, `playtime_secs`, `generation`; [`lib.rs`](../../../crates/rpcs3-control/src/lib.rs)), inside the owner so an ownerless placement carries none by type; the native driver now tells the TV when the same figure and owner are placed again under a new generation (AH.3's slot reset), still nothing for the same generation (AB.21's resume). The TV-to-server path ([`progress.rs`](../../../crates/guac-server/src/progress.rs)): `ProgressSink`, the latest-wins map per (profile, figure) behind a mutex, a `Notify` waking the drainer, nothing on the TV's side ever blocking but the quit; `Drainer`, spawned by `boot` on the server's runtime only when there's no `FigureFiles` hook; `ServerHandles` gains `progress` and `progression` (the experience table and the gold clamp, which AH.2 publishes; until then nothing is clamped and every level reads 0, unknown, so only gold and playtime writes announce themselves). Experience and gold are written at once, playtime once the sent total is 60 s past the row's (`PLAYTIME_WRITE_SECS`); an entry goes in whole on `send_final` (the figure left the portal), `flush` (the level ended) and `flush_and_wait` (the quit: `main` after the Bevy app returns, up to 3 s, and the farewell's fallback exit, 0.4 s, keeping its 5 s); values under an older generation, or for a row that's gone, are dropped and make no row; `FigureUpdated` goes out when the level or the gold changes and with each playtime write. The TV's half ([`progress.rs`](../../../crates/guacamole/src/progress.rs), `ProgressPlugin`, registered in `main`): per slot the owned figure and its values, sent when they change, sent whole when it leaves or another figure is placed over it; a figure put back under the same generation carries on from the TV's own values, which can be ahead of the row it was placed from (an orb collected the frame before it was lifted); a `LevelEnded` message asks for the flush. Nothing on the TV earns yet (AH.23 writes the values) and nothing ends a level yet, so the trigger split off as AH.33. A kill (Ctrl-C, SIGKILL) skips the quit's flush: experience and gold are already in, under a minute of playtime is lost. Unit tests, 31 new. The store's 8: fresh, and a second fresh after play untouched; one figure on two profiles two rows with their own generations; the caps clamping a write (100,000 and 70,000 to 96,500 and 65,000) and an edit, and `Caps::NONE` clamping nothing; a profile's delete taking its rows and not another's; values under the old generation dropped after an edit, after a reset (no row made) and after the row is made again; playtime kept unless told and the bump moving only the generation; a never-placed figure's first edit making its row; the database reopened keeping the row and the counter. The drainer's 13: the level at 0, 999, 1,000, 96,499 and 96,500 (1, 1, 2, 14, 15); 4 experience written and announcing nothing, then 1,000 announcing level 2; 2 gold announcing once and the same values again nothing; 59 one-second sends writing and announcing nothing, then the minute writing 60 and announcing once; a removal writing the 30 s waiting; the level's end writing it; an orb then an edit, and a coin then a reset, dropped, the fresh row left fresh; the caps at 96,500 and 65,000; a figure with no row writing nothing; 20,000 sends then a quit keeping the last values; a quit straight after a 1-point orb keeping it; a quit with no drainer returning at once; the map keeping the latest per figure with a removal sticking. Through the router and the worker, 3: a first placement's row fresh and carried, then earned, lifted and placed again with the row untouched and carried; two profiles' Eruptor two rows, each placement its own; a placement with no profile unlocked no row and no progress; and the Kaos swap's test now checks the swapped-in figure's row. Boot, 1: guacamole's boot drains a send through `ServerHandles` and its quit flush lands, the product's boot (its figure-file hook in place) runs no drainer. The driver, 1 (a new generation reaches the TV; the worker test checks the placement carries the fresh row). The TV, 5: nothing sent until the values change, then sent, then whole at the removal; an ownerless placement tracked as nothing; put back under the same generation carrying on from the TV's values and under a newer one from the row's; a figure placed over another sending the old one whole; `LevelEnded` asking for the flush. `guacamoles_set_has_the_products_tables_plus_settings` is now `..._plus_its_own`: the product's set has none of `figure_instances`, `figure_generation` and `settings`, guacamole's has the product's tables plus those three, and `the_store_runs_on_the_product_and_guacamole_sets` passes unchanged; AE.4's id-migration test copies only the migrations before its own now that one follows it. `cargo fmt --all --check` and the phone's clean, `cargo clippy --workspace --all-targets -- -D warnings` clean; `cargo test --workspace` 1,438 passed, 0 failed, 118 ignored, 90 s at a 1-minute load of 17.0-15.6 (an oracle lane and a fuzz run beside it); `openspec validate --all --strict` 9 of 9. e2e on the Mac, serial, `--skip ios_`, the phone built with `BUILD_TOKEN=e2e-test`: `E2E_APP=guacamole` 59 of 59 ok (46 for real: 16 TV, 5 intro, 25 shared; 13 product-only skips), 480 s at a 1-minute load of 9.4-14.2; the product (`SKYLANDER_PACK_ROOT` at the pack under `rpcs3/game_discs`) 56 of 59 ok, 31 for real (28 passed, 3 failed), 7 live-RPCS3 skips and 21 guacamole-only skips, 309 s at a 1-minute load of 8.8-19.8; its 3 failures are the three `.sky` tests the e2e README names as failing since the pre-AA base (`edit_level_and_gold_round_trips_through_stats_strip`, `gold_chevrons_jump_to_bounds_and_max_round_trips`, `resume_prompt_offers_prior_layout`), and AH.1 adds no e2e test, so the passes equal the base's 56; `docs/assets/screens/` restored. a value runs on into the next word as the scripts' bit-field macros read it); the difficulty, `console save params.option - difficulty` (bit 2,549, 4 bits, the `save val set`); the multiplier, `combat control data.accolade xp multiplier`, beside what sets it (`level progression data.accolade checks`' 10 xp multiplier accolades, each marked in the console save's `collection - accolades`, and the table); - [x] AH.5 O6, actors spawned mid-stretch (design decision 16), on AG.1, AG.34, AG.35 and AH.4: in the O-series behind `GUAC_ORACLE=1` ([`rpcs3-patches/oracle/`](../../../rpcs3-patches/oracle/)), nothing in it knowing the game: a verb arming a chain the harness names (a base address, pointer hops down to a list, the list's count and item offsets), keeping an item only when a word at an item offset matches a list the harness names (a name checksum or a vtable), following pointer hops per kept item (each link checked, an unreadable one recorded as missing with its bit) and copying named ranges into O5's ring where O5 samples (each flip's first pad read, or each game frame if AG.35 moves O5 there), bounded per record and per ring, the budgets stated here against the clearing's and the pit's actor counts and the stretch lengths AH.9 and AH.10 need. Rust: the codec in [`verbs.rs`](../../../tools/oracle-harness/src/verbs.rs), the fake server's handler and its loopback (the executable spec, run by `cargo test`), `oracle watch` taking chains, Giants' chain off AF.9's layout (the game's actor list, kept by filter: the Chompies, Norticus and what spawns (projectiles, orbs, coins, debris); each corpus's transform, velocity, placement and behaviour cell, and through a second hop its script variables: health, an orb's worth), the per-frame trace in `guac-oracle` keyed by actor; the MT's `_mti` and its first state word added to Giants' O5 list, and O5's range count stated (the hero's 24 and what joins it; the creatures ride O6's chain, not O5's). Unit tests in this box: every request, reply and refusal; a list growing and shrinking between flips; the filter keeping and dropping items; a two-hop item chain, and its second link broken read as missing; an item freed mid-chain; the budget at and one past its limit; a flip holding two game frames; the gate off answering `ERR unknown_cmd`. Verify, live on the Mac from AF.8's state: 600 flips of AF.11's walk script with O5's list and the chain armed and no pause, one record a flip (or a game frame), the chain's kept items equal to AF.9's reader filtered the same way at the end pause; the chained replays against fresh set A by AG.34's rule; the product's P1-P8 alone still applying as 8 commits; the identity line and the load average recorded here (AH.6's review, 2026-10-20: AH.6's metrics read four things this list leaves implicit, so the chain carries them too: the breakables in reach, placed from F0, with their health, behaviour cell or leaving (the chain's break and the drop count); the hero as an item, his health and the figure's experience and gold through AH.2's rows each record (bites, gains, totals); a clone's placement left `None`, though it carries its template's checksum; a dying actor's body readable to its last listing, or `fight_trace` refuses the replay. And, where the actor has one, a word that changes when its list slot is taken by another (`ChainItem::serial`): without it a slot freed and taken by another actor of the same kind between two records reads as one actor that jumped) (2026-10-20, M3 Max Mac: O6 rides O5's records and follows what spawns mid-stretch, nothing in it knowing the game; every part of the box landed and verified live, bar the chained replays' verdict by AG.34's rule, which misses one clause of its letter and is split as AH.40 for chotchki's call. **The verb** ([`0006-O6-*.patch`](../../../rpcs3-patches/oracle/0006-O6-oracle-chains-for-actors-spawned-mid-stretch-GUAC.patch), behind O3's gate): `CHAIN <slot> <items> <base> <path> <count> <array> <key> <values> [<range> ...]` -> `OK slot=<s> bytes=<b> listed=<n> kept=<k>` arms one of 8 chains before a watch (`ERR watching` while one is armed, `ERR no_list` when the path doesn't reach a list then, beside `bad_args`, `bad_slot`, `bad_items`, `bad_hop`, `bad_values`, `bad_range` and `too_big`); `CHAIN_CLEAR` drops them. From the base, pointer hops to a list (its count word and its array of item pointers; a count past 4,096 isn't a list); each item kept when its key matches one of up to 64 values, up to `<items>` (512 at most) a record; each kept item's ranges (32 at most, 1,024 bytes each) copied through hops of their own. A hop is `<off>` (the pointer there: not null, 4-aligned, readable), `<off>=<word>` (and the object it reaches starts with that word, a vtable) or `?<count>,<array>,<name>=<hash>`, a search of the list there for the element whose name hashes to the value (1,024 elements at most); a key is a word or `#<off>`, the FNV-1a of the C string that word points at. Giants' actors carry no name checksum (a placement's `_name` points into an interned pool, a clone's at its template's; `_ID` is the template's coin value, the corpus's `_timeStamp` the frame's game time, every node's `_handle` 20), so O6 hashes the name, and the search reaches a script variable by name in an instance that didn't exist when the chain was armed. `WATCH` takes the chains armed then and every record carries each one's block after O5's ranges, slot by slot: a 20-byte header (the status, 0 or the 1-based link that broke; listed; unread; matched; kept) and each kept item in list order (its address, list index, value index, missing mask and ranges), the rest zeros. `WATCH_CLEAR` keeps the chains and a new session drops them. **Budgets**: O5's ranges stay at 64 and 16 KiB a record; the chains get 48 KiB a record together; O5's ring grows from 16 MiB to 128 MiB and a `WATCH_READ` reply from 32 KiB of records to 256 KiB, so a whole record of both fits (the harness reads an older build's ring in its 32 KiB pages when the first big one is refused). Giants' three chains take 12,732 bytes a record (the actor list 48 items of 88 bytes, the orbs 64 of 96, the loot 32 of 72) beside O5's 5,216 (42 ranges with AH.4's fight cells and the generator's words; 37 ranges and 3,340 bytes without the fight's cells): 17,980 bytes with the header, 7,464 records (124 s of game frames) in the ring. The clearing's actor list held 166 at F0 and 167-199 through every walk; it's the level's list, so the pit's holds the same order, and O6 walks it whole each record and keeps the fight's (the hero, 4 Chompies, Norticus' 2 placements, the 13 breakables in reach, the 9 debris templates, Eruptor's projectiles at a lob every 0.25 s living 2 s: under 48); a Chompy's 4 experience makes 4 or 5 orbs (AH.4's 1.1), 16-20 for the fight against 64. AH.9's stretches (a throw to its coin, a breakable hit until it breaks) and AH.10's (from the wake to the last Chompy, standing still about 15 s until Eruptor's defeat) fit the 124 s ring with room; AF.11's 1,800-flip walk took 29.0 MB of it (set O, without the fight's cells). **Giants' chains** ([`chains.rs`](../../../tools/oracle-harness/src/chains.rs)): the actor list off AF.9's layout (the application, its insight core, the updater list, the `tfbActorUpdateGame` at its index there (3 in AF.8's state), its `_gameActorList`, each hop's vtable checked), a corpus kept by its placement's name hash (the hero by his; `Enemy_Chompy(1)`-`(4)`; Norticus' two placements; the 13 breakables, AG.7's reach's 12 and the ledge route's `barrel_b_intact(4)` (its other two among the 12); the barrels', pots' and gourds' debris templates; Eruptor's `009_LavaProjectile`, `_Upgrade`, `009_FireBlob`, `009_FireBlobSplash`, `009_LavaAttack` and `009_MiniVolcano`), a clone told by its `_parent`, each with its transform, velocity, placement, parent, behaviour cell, `_activityState`, `_activeTime`, and by name through its `_localVarList` its health (`description.creature info.hp`) and alerted flag (`description.attention parameters.alerted`); the experience orbs, which aren't actors: each one a structure in `combat control data.xp ball control.all active balls` (a `global.bld` export, found in the handle table), its worth `xp ball.xp val`, its serial the game's own `xp ball.my ID` (`Next Ball ID` counted up for every ball) and its `DeathXP_NewOrbOfficial_*` particle's transform, read whether `xp ball` is a structure or a reference; and the loot in `Macros_UI`'s `LootInPlay` set, its `lootActor`'s transform and velocity, its `monetaryValue` and the actor's placement, parent and name. A record decodes into `guac_oracle`'s `ChainRecord`s, `MtWords` from the generator's `_mti` and first word now on Giants' O5 list, the hero's item carrying O5's fight cells (his health, experience and gold through AH.2's rows) beside his chain's behaviour, a clone's placement `None` and its serial counting its slot taken again (its placement pointer changing or its `_activeTime` going back; Giants' actors carry no id or spawn count); `GiantsChains::names` gives `fight_trace` each kind's behaviour cells named at the end pause; `evaluate` is O6's walk over guest memory read through PINE, the reference a block is checked against. The distribution tests (rank-sum, chi-square for discrete values, the Bonferroni threshold over a metric list, leave-one-out over a reference); decision 17's equivalence check over its named list. Verify: `cargo test --workspace` (2026-10-20, M3 Max Mac: pure, in [`guac_oracle::fight`](../../../crates/guac-oracle/src/fight/mod.rs) and [`guac_oracle::stats`](../../../crates/guac-oracle/src/stats.rs), the crate still `#![forbid(unsafe_code)]` with no I/O. **The trace both sides fill** ([`ticks.rs`](../../../crates/guac-oracle/src/fight/ticks.rs)): a `Tick` a game frame (frames and game seconds since F0, the hero's pad, the MT's watched words) holding `Actor`s, each with its kind, placement, pivot, facing, velocity, behaviour and clip by name and script variables by name; `FightTrace::new` refuses a lost frame, time going back, an id twice in a tick, one changing kind or coming back after leaving, then names every actor alike on both sides: a placement by its name, a spawn by its parent (the nearest actor of a `SpawnRule`'s kinds a tick before: `Enemy_Chompy(2)>orb#3`), spawns in one tick numbered by where they appear, never by a run's own ids. A `Cue` (a behaviour, a clip, a variable at or under a value, leaving) is how a rule names what AH.9 and AH.10 find marks a throw, a lunge or a break. **What O6 must fill** (O6 doesn't exist yet; AH.5's reader fills these types, [`oracle.rs`](../../../crates/guac-oracle/src/fight/oracle.rs)): a `ChainRecord` riding each O5 record (its flip, read and `_curFrame`, refused out of step) with its kept `ChainItem`s (the list entry's address, the filter's kind, the placement its name checksum resolves to, the body as `recording::Body` or `None` when a hop failed, the raw behaviour cell, script variables by name, the missing mask) and `MtWords` (`_mti` and the state's first word, which AH.5 adds to Giants' O5 list); `Names`, built from the extract, names each kind's behaviour cells and the hero's clip ids. `fight_trace` keys game time by AG.1's `Recording::game_time` under `Alignment::Earlier` (`Clock::step` over records paired by `_curFrame`, a lost or repeated frame refused), joins a `hero` chain item to O5's hero, takes an address gone and back as a new actor, and refuses an unreadable body or an unnamed cell. **Draws and the horizon** ([`draws.rs`](../../../crates/guac-oracle/src/fight/draws.rs)): from the F0 state (an `rng peek`, or `sgenrand` of an `rng seed`), stepping its twists until the first word matches counts every draw since F0, a burst past 624 in one frame included, where `_mti` alone can't; `anchor_horizon` is AF.10's `usable_horizon` over the counts at `tolerance::COUNT`'s floor 0, on the frames every replay holds. **The extractors** ([`extract.rs`](../../../crates/guac-oracle/src/fight/extract.rs)), each over either side's trace and reading only the cells a rule names: `chain` (press → throw → ball → burst → break → coin, a link per throw), `states` (behaviours with entry stamps), `bites` (the lunge's hit by reach or by the biter's own variable changing, with the health lost and how many landed that tick, so a turned-away bite counts), `pushes` (speed runs over AG.8's `Motion`, length in game seconds), `pickups` and `gains` (orbs and coins collected or not, each change to the hero's experience and gold), `breaks` and `drop_count`, `totals` (AF.10's `Totals`) and `logs`. **The logs** ([`log.rs`](../../../crates/guac-oracle/src/fight/log.rs)): one per actor; the reference is each actor's agreed prefix over the replays with its stamp bands and, where they part, the branches counted; a `Judge` holds floors in game seconds (per kind, else a default), the horizon (a band past it, and any kind a roll times, checked by order alone); `leave_one_out` and `floors_needed` are AF.12's calibration on game time. **Distributions** ([`stats.rs`](../../../crates/guac-oracle/src/stats.rs)): the rank-sum moved here unchanged from the harness's divergence report, which (with `observing`, AG.34's rule) now imports it; its two-sided p; chi-square for fit and for two samples' counts over the values seen; `Family`, Bonferroni (0.05 over five metrics fails at |z| 2.5758); `Distribution` judging a candidate against the replays pooled and each replay against the rest. The normal and chi-square tails are both the regularized incomplete gamma, good to about 1e-14. **Equivalence** ([`equivalence.rs`](../../../crates/guac-oracle/src/fight/equivalence.rs)): decision 17's list, the pose per component at `tolerance::POSITION` and `ANGLE` (angles wrapped), the rest equal or not. Reused, not forked: AG.1's `Recording::game_time`, AG.8's `Motion`, AF.10's `usable_horizon` and `Totals`, AF.12's `COUNT`, `POSITION` and `ANGLE`, AF.8's `Mt19937`. **Found**: game time starts at a trace's first tick, and the oracle's first step is the pause clamped to 0.05 s (AG.1), so a headless run judged against it has to step the oracle's recorded steps (AG.18's model replay) or every stamp sits 1/30 s early; the module docs say so. The rank-sum keeps AF.11's lack of a tie correction (conservative on heavily tied samples; a bite's value goes to chi-square). Unit tests, 28 (`guac-oracle` 61 → 89), on synthetic traces with known answers: two replays whose Chompies interleave differently, each actor's log agreeing, passing (`chompies_interleaving_differently_pass_while_each_log_agrees`, where AF's interleaved prefix parts at its first event on the same replays, and from O5 records `replays_interleaving_differently_on_game_time_pass_and_one_actor_off_fails`: one replay hitched, the order swapped and the frames 1-3 apart, a headless run at the nominal step passing); one actor's log off, failing (that one's late Chompy 2, and `one_actors_log_off_fails_that_actor_alone`: a late stamp, a state skipped, one too many, an actor missing or extra); a lost frame refused (`a_trace_refuses_what_it_cant_key`, and through `fight_trace`); a hitch in game time (a 0.2 s frame counted as 0.05 in `a_recording_and_its_chain_make_named_ticks_on_game_time`; `a_push_lasts_its_game_time_a_hitch_included`, 0.15 s in 9 steps or 7); the horizon at the first disagreeing draw count (frame 5, parted at 6, one replay drawing once more in frame 5; with `draws_are_counted_across_twists_and_bursts` and the refusals of a reseeded, out-of-reach or backward generator); a turned-away bite counted (290 → 290 then 290 → 278, by reach and by the biter's counter alike, and two in one tick sharing 24); rank-sum and chi-square at their thresholds (U 75, z 1.8898, passes and U 76, z 1.9654, fails at 0.05, both pass Bonferroni's five; 7.30 passes and 7.84 fails at 3 degrees of freedom; the tails against their tables); a reference passing a candidate inside it and failing one a floor outside (2 frames' floor over a 79-80 band: 77-82 in, 76 and 83 out), its distribution twin; equivalence passing, and failing on each of its 9 fields alone; and the horizon and rolled kinds by order, branches, leave-one-out and its floors, the chain, pickups, states, naming and an address reused. Bent 36 ways ([`bend.py`](/Users/chotchki/workspace/guacamole-research/ah6-metrics/bend.py), results beside it), each failing at least one test: one interleaved log for all actors, stamps unjudged, the floor dropped, the horizon or the rolled kinds ignored, leave-one-out keeping the left-out run (logs and distributions), a lost tick let through, the oracle's time raw, by frame or summed past a gap, spawns numbered by id or parented by any kind, a twist ignored, the horizon at a floor of 1, a bite only when health drops or at the lunge's last tick, a push timed in steps, a one-sided p, no Bonferroni split, a degree of freedom too many, each equivalence field dropped and the wrap, every gone orb collected, breaks counted as drops, a break taken out of reach, the first press for the last, a late first read as an entry. Six of those first passed and their tests were sharpened (the distribution leave-one-out now pins the statistic against the rest; the chain test gained a re-press and a coinless break out of reach; the states test an actor unread at F0; the naming test a nearer pot); load 1.79-3.96 throughout. Gates: `cargo fmt --all --check` and clippy `--workspace --all-targets -D warnings` clean (1-minute load 4.84 → 5.32); `cargo test --workspace --no-fail-fast` 1,659 passed, 0 failed, 139 ignored, 78 s, load 5.13 → 5.02; the game-magic guard over the staged files 18 passed; `openspec validate --all --strict` 9 of 9; guac-server, guacamole and the phone untouched, so no e2e. The design doc's The oracle and CLAUDE.md's `guac-oracle` line are AH.32's Review (2026-10-20, M3 Max Mac, the AH.37, AH.6 and AH.22 review): the box's synthetic cases recomputed apart from the crate ([`ah6_recompute.py`](/Users/chotchki/workspace/guacamole-research/ahx-review/scripts/ah6_recompute.py): pairwise U, scipy 1.18.1's tails, an MT19937 and a naming and extractor pass written for the review), 63 checks, every one the crate's answer. The thresholds against scipy: U 75 and 76 at z 1.889822 and 1.965415, either side of 0.05 two-sided and both inside Bonferroni's five (`norm.isf(0.005)` 2.575829); chi-square 7.84 (p 0.0494) and 7.30 at 3 degrees of freedom against `chi2.isf(0.05, 3)` 7.814728; 50/50 against 30/70 8.3333 on 1 (`chi2_contingency`, no correction). The crate's own tails against scipy over z 0-9 and x 0.01-80 at 1-30 degrees of freedom: 7.7e-15 relative at worst for the normal, 2.3e-14 for chi-square; `normal_quantile` 4.4e-9 off at p 1e-9 (bisection on 1 − Q), far closer at the thresholds. The missing tie correction: scipy's tie-corrected z gives the same verdicts on the box's samples (1.9189, 1.9880), and over 2,000 random pairs reads |z| at most 0.31 % higher on 30 waits a side quantized to 1/60 s and 6.05 % on four-valued samples (chi-square's), so conservative by a hair where the box uses it. The draws: the review's MT19937 counts the box's runs (18 a frame, a burst of 1,500, 700) from `sgenrand(4357)` and from 10,554 advanced 1,000 to the same totals by twisting until word 0 matches, refuses the reseeded, out-of-reach and backward cases and puts the horizon at 5, parted at 6. Seven bends of my own past the box's 36 ([`mutate_ah6.py`](/Users/chotchki/workspace/guacamole-research/ahx-review/scripts/mutate_ah6.py)): six passed every test at first (a band's stamps judged when its first replay's frame is inside the horizon rather than its last; a break in the burst's own tick not the ball's; an address keeping its actor across kinds; a spawn's parent sought in its own tick rather than the one before, which loses a breakable leaving as its coin comes out; the horizon from the earliest replay's start; a pickup by ground distance), and the first five now fail: `past_the_horizon_and_for_rolled_kinds_only_order_counts` at horizon 79, `a_barrel_gone_in_the_bursts_own_tick_is_the_balls_and_parents_its_coin`, `a_slot_taken_between_records_is_a_new_actor_by_kind_or_serial` and a replay starting two frames in; the sixth stands, the spec not saying which distance an orb's 20 ft is. **O6's shape** carries every metric once AH.5 fills four things its box left implicit, now noted there: the breakables in reach, placed from F0, which the filter's "what spawns" leaves out and `breaks`, the chain's break and the drop count read; the hero as an item (his health, the figure's experience and gold through AH.2's rows: `bites`, `gains` and `totals` read them); a clone's placement left `None` though it carries its template's checksum, or every orb is named by the template; a dying actor's body readable to its last listing, since `fight_trace` refuses a whole recording on one unreadable body. One it couldn't tell at all: a slot freed and taken by another actor of the same kind between two records (an orb collected as another spawns) read as one actor that jumped, so `ChainItem` gains `serial`, a word O6 copies that changes on reuse, the same address under another serial a new actor (the same test). Docs: the naming's tie-break (spawns alike in position and velocity fall to the run's ids, twins whose names can swap between runs, which the module claimed never happened) and `BiteRule` (the hit's marker has to land in the tick the health drops, or every bite reads as turned away: AH.10's choice). `guac-oracle` 89 → 91 tests. Unit tests in this box: every op class written and read back field for field; a graph round-tripped through the writer and the readers; a skip past the end of its op array, an index out of range and an unknown class each refused by name. `#[ignore]`d disc test: every script in the four files AH reads parses, with each class's count recorded and the disc's census (aj-script: 14,207 scripts, 44 classes) reproduced. Verify: the disc test run on the Mac with wall time and load average; the fuzz targets 60 s each seeded, the hours left to AH.0 (2026-10-20, M3 Max Mac: [`script.rs`](../../../crates/guac-formats/src/script.rs) in `guac-formats`, a new module (`ops`, `builder`, `tests`) plus registrations: `lib.rs`, 35 `TYPES` lines (29 op classes, three of their bases and three stacks, sizes from ac4's registry), two registry entries (`script_ops`, `script_graph`), two fuzz targets and `IgzBuilder::external_name`. **The layouts:** `Class` lists the 44 in aj-script's census order, each with its registry size and whether it opens a block or is a flow, both checked against `TYPES`' parent chains; AE.2's eight layouts serve as they are (`OpCode` for the ten classes with no fields of their own), AH.2's five op layouts (`OpSetValue`, `OpIncValue`, `OpCheckValue`, `OpSetReference`, `OpDefineMacroSpecialization`) moved here from `progression/ops.rs` (its `ScriptStructure` stays: not an op) and 22 are new, with the value and group stacks; `Op::read` decodes an object by its class and refuses any other type by name, and `Op::fields` lists every registry field by its name. **The metaobject-index resolution:** `Graph::read` resolves every class word it reads (a literal's `_type`, a variable's container and contents, `_editType`) through EXNM, flagged or not, and refuses one past the table (out of range, the field named) or outside `metaobject`; a contents or edit type may also be a local definition (read as an op) or a `global.bld` import. **The graph:** every `tfbScriptInfo`'s positions, then what they reach, each object decoded once: operands through expressions and `_otherPoint`, the variables, definitions (stubs included) and `ValueInfo`s their paths name, `_macroDef`s and local contents types; `_masterVarList` stays a ref for AH.14. Refused by name besides: an op at a position outside the 44 classes, a `_branchPC` past its list, a block on an op that opens none, 0xFFFF off a flow, an operand that's neither a stack nor an expression, and more list entries than one per 2 data bytes (lists sharing their words, a fuzz shape: a file Alchemy writes holds one per 4). **The writer** (`script::builder::Writer`, `test-fixtures`), every offset a registry literal: objects, words and class words, paths (locals, engine members by FNV-1a of the lowercased name, `global.bld` ids, named objects), every stack kind, literals, expressions, variables, the shared flows, positions and blocks, and helpers for a script's frame (PRESCRIPT, STARTUP, SHUTDOWN, MACROS), behaviours, set, inc, check and else, loops, define and call, spawn, start sequence, set behaviour and remove; `creature()` (a Chompy-shaped script, our numbers) and `every_class()` are the seeds, and the e2e fixtures' scripts (AH.24, AH.25, AH.27) are written with it when they land. **AH.2 on it:** the save-param table's body stepping matches on `Op::read`, so the two readers share one decoder; AH.2's 10 unit tests pass unchanged and its disc test reruns to the same table, 24 rows and clamp 65,000 in 233 steps (432 ms parsed, 53 ms read). The property harness runs both readers within their heap budgets. **The `#[ignore]`d disc test** [`giants_script_ops`](../../../crates/guac-formats/tests/giants_script_ops.rs), release: the five files AH reads, every script PRESCRIPT first (`level_213_tutotial.bld` 221 scripts, 53,497 positions, 25,351 op objects at them, 43 classes; `global.bld` 58, 81,783, 45,572, 43; `init_setup.bld` 11, 9,380, 4,375, 30; `009_eruptor.bld` 8, 3,385, 2,022, 38; `bootstrap.bld` 1, 27, 20, 16; between them all 44, each class's counts in `ah-vm/out/ah13_ah_files.txt`), 3.2 s at a 1-minute load of 2.3; the whole disc, **aj-script's census reproduced**: 956 files, 893 archives, 5,801 IGZ, 595 with scripts, **14,207 scripts, 44 classes**, 4,617,339 positions, 2,308,176 op objects at them, every class's positions and objects equal to aj-script's table, 1,713,505 operands, 0 failures; the unflagged class words over aj-script's walk 1,485,888, every one in range and in `metaobject`, and the graph reads 1,208 more past that walk (990 RHS reference stacks, 171 RHS value stacks, 47 variables: what parameters at no position name, reached through the paths that name those parameters); aj-script's 2,596,478 op objects are the 44 classes' 2,591,901 and 4,577 `OpParameter`s, a class no list holds and no path names; 147.6 s, 2 min 28 s wall with the build, at a 1-minute load of 2.6 then 2.3. the tick; blocks and the close rule; a script's frame (PRESCRIPT's dispatch, STARTUP once, SHUTDOWN, behaviours, `set behavior` this tick from PRESCRIPT, next tick from anywhere else); checks and flows; loops; macros (one frame per definition by name, the busy guard skipping the whole use block, the arguments read in the caller's context and bound by name then id, the body skipping its own INTERFACE, the return to right after the caller's INTERFACE with the callee's frame still readable, the frame reset when the use block closes, specializations); variables, structures and sets; values and arithmetic (single precision, never double); the comparison codes and the 1e-4 tolerance; literals and rolls (`random(N)` drawn through the host, AH.15's generator); members; strict and play mode, the budget and the whitelist; what's left to the host and what isn't modelled yet, each listed. **Linking** ([`program.rs`](../../../crates/guac-script/src/program.rs), `Program::link`): the files loaded together (bootstrap, global, a level or character file) into ids, every op, operand and file variable an op list reaches decoded once (on demand through `guac_formats::script` past what the graph read), `global.bld` imports through the export ids, stubs to the placed definition of their name and kind. **The host trait** ([`host.rs`](../../../crates/guac-script/src/host.rs)): `Host::get`, `set` and `reset` per `Access` (the placement asking, the receiver or none for a path's head, the member by its import hashes, the `_otherPoint` read), `draw` (the session generator's next word) and `op`, a world op or `find variable` with its operands read, answered done, run (with a receiver and what it reads as on a path) or no target; a `HostFault` is nothing (a write drops, a check skips) or unmodelled. `Members` holds each modelled member's registered kind, bounds and default and whether the host or a value cell keeps it; every write converts and clamps first (`convert`). The VM's own members are `my`, `null`, `undefined`, `its`, `valuedereferencer`, `setvariant.count`/`first`/`last`/`random` and `system.cast to integer` (an integer cell, so a cast truncates). The tests' hosts are plain structs; guacamole's `SystemParam` host is the member boxes'. **Strict** returns a `Halt` naming the placement, its script, the position, the op and the member (by name when `Members` knows it, else its hashes); **play** stops that placement's script for good, logs it and runs the rest. **The budget** is `DEFAULT_BUDGET`, 250,000 ops a placement a tick, over twice the largest tick measured (below). **The whitelist** lists placements by name or script; any other stays inert and is listed (`Vm::inert`). **The stall detector** ([`stall.rs`](../../../crates/guac-script/src/stall.rs)) fires once per stay past a behaviour's longest recorded one, and only for behaviours with a limit. Two readings the disc settled, outside the tree (`ah-vm/probe14`, `out/ah14_scoping_*.txt`, `ah14_specs.txt`): a variable heading a path inside a macro body is in no script's `_masterVarList` (6,640 in `init_setup` and `global`) and one heading a path in a script's own ops always is (58 and 1,914), so locals and frames are made by name on first use and `_masterVarList` isn't read; and a call's parameters match the definition's by name and id alike (all 21,873 whose definition is in the same file), while a specialization's parameters, its own INTERFACE's included, name its base as `_macroDef` (723 specializations, each with an INTERFACE of its own, their 1,650 parameters all naming the base; `out/ah14_spec_params.txt`), so a parameter naming the base reads the running specialization's frame. **The `#[ignore]`d disc test** [`giants_toys`](../../../crates/guac-script/tests/giants_toys.rs), release: parsed in 115 ms, linked in 26 ms (70 scripts, 52,655 ops, 40,516 operands, 2,690 definitions, 343 file variables). Against aj-vm's 57,418: every macro's call count is equal but two, `Set Save Param` 1,282 against 1 and `Save - Bit Field - Set Bit Field` 1,650 against 189, because aj-vm's tool reads a specialization's parameters from its base's frame, which nothing binds, so `Set to Default Param`'s loop over `fields total` read 0 there (`ah-vm/out/ah14_tick_discover.txt`). **Throughput**, AH.35's starting point: the first tick 14.98 ms median over 21 fresh VMs (14.67-15.18), 7.5 M op executions a second; the macro alone 6.9 M; 2.3 s wall with the build at a 1-minute load of 5.3 (another lane working beside it). The arenas never free a set, structure or cell, unmeasured over a session: AH.38. **Review (2026-10-20, the AH.12-AH.15 review):** a specialization's own parameter read the base's frame, never bound, so 0, while the specialization bound a nested call's arguments or ran a nested call's after-return code: `param_frame` looked only at the newest call, against the crate docs' own rule (the running specialization's frame). - [x] AH.15 The generator (design decision 11), on AH.14: MT19937 with the 1998 seeding and `random(N)` (a whole number on [0, N] with both ends, a fraction on [0, N)), seeded from the clock per session and fixed in tests, checked against `guac_oracle::rng`. Unit tests in this box: the reference generator's published output; `random(3)` over a seeded run reaching 0 and 3 and never 4; `random(0.5)` never 0.5; a state from an `rng peek` loaded and continuing as `guac_oracle::rng` does; two sessions differing. Verify: `cargo test --workspace` (2026-10-20, M3 Max Mac: [`generator.rs`](../../../crates/guac-script/src/generator.rs) in `guac-script`, `Generator` and `session_seed`, its semantics in the module's docs and the crate docs' Literals and rolls, `guac-oracle` a dev-dependency only. **The seeding** is `sgenrand` as Matsumoto and Nishimura's `mt19937int.c` of 1999/10/28 has it (two `69069x + 1` steps a word: what the design and AF.8 call the 1998 seeding), and that file's published output for `sgenrand(4357)` pins it, so the generator is checked against a published vector of the game's own seeding, not only the 2002 one (`guac_oracle::rng`'s test calls 2,867,219,139 self-generated; it's that file's first number). The file of 1998/4/6 seeds with one full `69069x` step a word and gives another stream (3,510,405,877 first). Both files and their outputs are outside the tree (`ah-vm/ref15/`). **The session seed** is the wall clock's nanoseconds folded to 32 bits plus the process's count of seeds made times an odd constant, so two sessions started in one tick of the clock still differ; it's the crate's one read of the world outside it. Nothing in guacamole makes a generator yet: the host that hands the VM its draws is the member boxes' (AH.16 on). **A corner the box's [0, N) doesn't hold:** the float roll rounds the drawn number to single precision before scaling it (the original's routine as the decompile's types read it), so the 128 highest numbers read r itself, one draw in 2^25. `random_literal` keeps it for parity and its test now pins it (0xFFFFFF7F reads 0.5 − 2^-25; 0xFFFFFF80 and up read 0.5). AF.8's table and script-runtime's Random rolls say up to but not including N; nothing measured sees one draw in 33 million, so the wording is chotchki's call, not a box. chotchki (2026-10-20): spec it to match the game; script-runtime's Random rolls now says a fraction reaches N once in 2^25 draws, with a scenario on the corner. Unit tests, 10 (9 in `generator`, 1 through the VM), all seeded: the 1999 file's published output (the first five, the 624th and 625th either side of the second twist, the 1000th); the 2002 vector (5489's first and 10,000th) through this crate's twist and tempering, its seeding loaded from `guac_oracle`; seven seeds word for word and 3,127 draws each against `guac_oracle::rng::Mt19937::sgenrand`; a peek's state loaded and continuing 2,000 draws as `guac_oracle`'s does (the AF.5 state's numbers, `_mti` 617, and the census's other two seeds at 624 and 623; a twisted block at 0 for 700 draws), 625 drawn and 623 words refused; `random(3)` over 100,000 rolls of seed 5150 reaching 0 to 3 and never 4 (24,957, 24,995, 24,912 and 25,136 times); `random(0.5)` over a million rolls never 0.5 (4.99e-7 to 0.4999996, none of the 128 corner numbers in the run); script-runtime's Chompy bites, 11 + `random(3)` a thousand times on four seeds, each value 226 to 271 times, inside the spec's 209-291; a roll of anything but a number drawing nothing; two sessions' seeds and first 64 draws differing; and a script's `bite = 11 + random(3)` and `frac = random(0.5)` over 1,000 ticks drawing from the host's generator one number a roll in the order written, each equal to the generator's own `random`. Throughput: 10 M draws in 14 ms release, 176 ms dev. Gates: `cargo fmt --all --check` and clippy `-D warnings` clean; `cargo test --workspace --no-fail-fast` 1,564 passed, 0 failed, 133 ignored, 58 s at a 1-minute load of 3.3; `openspec validate --all --strict` 9 of 9. Review (2026-10-20): a Python MT19937 written apart from both crates, with the 1999 `sgenrand`, reproduces the published file's 1,000 numbers, and from it seed 5150's `random(3)` counts, its million `random(0.5)` rolls' 4.99e-7 to 0.4999996 and the four seeds' bites (226 to 271) are the ones above (`ah-vm/review/`))

scripts and outputs in [`ah60/`](/Users/chotchki/workspace/guacamole-research/ah60/) and the aim's readers in `ah50/scripts/`. **The oracle's lob** turns him from 202.21° to the Chompy the autoaim picks ((1) in 8 of the first ten, to 181.82-181.89°, and in `fight-03-s20261024`; (2) in `fight-01` and `fight-04`, to 200.6°, and in the other nine seeded, to 195.96-201.16°; the nearest by his pivot not always the one picked) and leads it: 50.99-51.67 ft/s at 65° at (1) (`lead_fit2.py`: the target's flight-time lead alone gives 46-47, the script's own numbers the rest), `fight-00` bursting at F0 + 122, 4.830 m across from his pivot, beating (1) and (2). **The cause**: Giants' `Calc - Tangent` and `Calc - Cosine` are specializations of `Calc - Sin` whose INTERFACEs hold the same parameter ops; each calls `Calc - Sin` and reads its `result` after the return, which our VM read as the caller's own (0), so `Calc - Ballistics - Get Velocity FROM Constant Angle` found no solution: the lob flew at the script's 80 ft/s default (AH.19's 74 ft/s a frame on) and the aim turned him nowhere (in `fight-00`'s scene 76.99 ft/s at its first record, his facing held at 202.21°, bursting 9.72 m across on (4)). **Fix**, in `guac-script` ([`vm.rs`](../../../crates/guac-script/src/vm.rs) `Call.returned`, `param_frame`; the crate docs' Macros): in a call's after-return code a parameter naming the callee's definition, or the base it specializes, reads the callee's frame even where the caller declares it too. Tried first and dropped: the family's parameters in one frame (the base's), which fixed the trig as well, but resetting them as a sibling's call closed cut the projectile manager's `target hit logic` loop (one `Projectile - Logic` specialization calling another) to its first target, sparing `fight-02-s20261023`'s (2) 7.1 ft from the burst that the oracle's beats; frames stay per definition, as the crate docs had them. A figure placed with no profile earns too, fresh, is sent nowhere and keeps nothing once it leaves (figure-progress: earns in play but keeps nothing once it leaves the portal). A waiting figure's slot reset (the same figure placed again under a newer generation) makes its slot's values the fresh row's, so its tag at its join is fresh. ## Workflow follow-up

- Archive only after AA and AG have archived: AH's figure-cards delta REMOVES a requirement AA's unarchived delta adds, and its pad-input delta adds to AG's unarchived capability, so `openspec archive` would refuse AH first. AH.0 checks the removed requirement's name against AA's archived text.
- AE.13's turntables, AG.29's level and AH.26's HUD and screen change the same TV; whichever lands second keeps the others' tests passing.
